    function qfCount305(v){

    }
    function qfAnchorSearch305(html){

    }

    function qfAbsNum306(v){
        var s=String(v==null?'':v).replace(/,/g,'').replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,'');if(!s)return '';
        var m=s.match(/^([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*(w|W|k|K|万|千)?$/);if(!m)return '';
        var n=Number(m[1]);if(!isFinite(n)||n<0)return '';
        var u=m[2]||'';if(u==='w'||u==='W'||u==='万')n*=10000;else if(u==='k'||u==='K'||u==='千')n*=1000;
        return String(Math.round(n));
    }
    function qfPlain306(html){
        return String(html||'').replace(/<script[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi,' ').replace(/<style[\s\S]*?<\/style>/gi,' ').replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi,'\n').replace(/<\/p>/gi,'\n').replace(/<[^>]+>/g,' ').replace(/&nbsp;/gi,' ').replace(/&amp;/gi,'&').replace(/&#39;/gi,"'").replace(/&quot;/gi,'"').replace(/[ \t]+/g,' ').replace(/\n\s+/g,'\n');

    function qfServerLimitBool313(v){
        if(v===true||v===1)return true;if(v===false||v===0)return false;
        var s=String(v==null?'':v).trim().toLowerCase();
        if(!s)return null;
        if(/^(?:1|true|yes|y|on|可用|适用|限免|免费|参与)$/.test(s))return true;
        if(/^(?:0|false|no|n|off|不可用|不适用|非限免|不参与)$/.test(s))return false;
        return null;
    }
    function qfServerLimitAnalyze313(root){
        var out={known:false,eligible:false,evidence:''},q=[{v:root,p:'',d:0}],seen=[],steps=0;

    function qfNormHtml304(html){
        return String(html||'').replace(/&quot;/gi,'"').replace(/&#34;/g,'"').replace(/\\"/g,'"').replace(/\u0022/gi,'"').replace(/\u003c/gi,'<').replace(/\u003e/gi,'>');
    }
    function qfScalar304(html,keys){

            var key304=String(ks[i304]).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&');

                new RegExp('["\']?'+key304+'["\']?\\s*[:=]\\s*["\']?([0-9]+(?:\\.[0-9]+)?(?:万)?)','i'),
                new RegExp('\\\"'+key304+'\\\"\\s*:\s*\\\"?([0-9]+(?:\\.[0-9]+)?(?:万)?)','i')
            ];
            for(var j304=0;j304<rr304.length;j304++){var m304=s.match(rr304[j304]);if(m304&&m304[1])return String(m304[1]);}
        }
        return '';
    }

    function qfServerLimitBool313(v){
        if(v===true||v===1)return true;if(v===false||v===0)return false;

        if(/^(?:1|true|yes|y|on|可用|适用|限免|免费|参与)$/.test(s))return true;
        if(/^(?:0|false|no|n|off|不可用|不适用|非限免|不参与)$/.test(s))return false;
        return null;
    }

        var s=String(v==null?'':v).replace(/,/g,'').replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,'');if(!s)return '';

        return String(Math.round(n));
    }

    function qfServerLimitBool313(v){
        if(v===true||v===1)return true;if(v===false||v===0)return false;

        if(!s)return null;
        if(/^(?:1|true|yes|y|on|可用|适用|限免|免费|参与)$/.test(s))return true;
        if(/^(?:0|false|no|n|off|不可用|不适用|非限免|不参与)$/.test(s))return false;
        return null;
    }
    function qfServerLimitAnalyze313(root){
        var out={known:false,eligible:false,evidence:''},q=[{v:root,p:'',d:0}],seen=[],steps=0;

function qfOrderAllowDecodeV310(encoded){

    }catch(_e){}

}

    for(var j=0;j<allow.length;j++)if(out.indexOf(allow[j])<0)out.push(allow[j]);
    return out;
}

/* ============================================================
 * 情无登录 · alpha52
 * 完全采用用户附件「📖起点中文」的登录方式：
 * 1. email/password POST 站点根目录 /auth.php?action=login
 * 2. 保存 Set-Cookie + request_token 到 source.loginHeader/loginInfo
 * 3. 使用 /qd/auth.php?action=me 验证
 * 网页登录同样直接复刻附件 webLogin，只增加自定义域名。
 * ============================================================ */
function qfQwNativeNormBaseV52(v){
    var s=String(v||"").trim();
    if(!s)return "";
    if(!/^https?:\/\//i.test(s))s="http://"+s;
    s=s.replace(/[?#].*$/g,"").replace(/\/+$/g,"");
    var m=s.match(/^(https?:\/\/[^\/]+)(\/.*)?$/i);
    if(!m)return s;
    var host=m[1],path=String(m[2]||"");
    var q=path.match(/^(.*?\/qd)(?:\/.*)?$/i);
    if(q)return host+q[1];
    if(!path||path==="/")return host+"/qd";
    return s;
}
function qfQwNativeBaseV52(){

        if(v!=null&&String(v).trim())x=qfQwNativeNormBaseV52(v);
    }catch(e0){}
    if(!x){try{x=qfQwNativeNormBaseV52(source.get("qf_qw_base")||"");}catch(e1){}}
    return x||d;
}

                    if(tx&&tx.length>=10&&!/创作的.{0,20}(?:小说|作品)《/.test(tx))return tx;
                }
            }
        }catch(_j301){}
        var pats=[
            /<div[^>]+id=["']book-intro-detail["'][^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/div>/i,
            /<div[^>]+class=["'][^"']*book-intro[^"']*["'][^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/div>/i
        ];
        for(var pi=0;pi<pats.length;pi++){
            var m=html.match(pats[pi]);if(!m||!m[1])continue;
            var t=String(m[1]).replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi,'\n').replace(/<\/p>/gi,'\n').replace(/<[^>]+>/g,'').replace(/&nbsp;/gi,' ').replace(/&amp;/gi,'&').replace(/\s*\n\s*/g,'\n').replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,'');
            if(t.length>=10)return t;
        }
        /* 现代起点详情经常把作品简介放在 hydration JSON / script，而不是静态 DOM。 */
        var ks=['BookIntro','bookIntro','BookIntroWords','bookIntroWords','BookDesc','bookDesc','BookDescription','bookDescription','qdBookDescription','QDBookDescription','Introduction','introduction'];
        function unq(s){
            s=String(s||'');
            try{return JSON.parse('"'+s.replace(/"/g,'\\"')+'"');}catch(_u){}
            return s.replace(/\\n/g,'\n').replace(/\\r/g,'').replace(/\\t/g,' ').replace(/\\\//g,'/').replace(/\\u([0-9a-fA-F]{4})/g,function(_,h){try{return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h,16));}catch(_e){return _;}});
        }
        for(var ki=0;ki<ks.length;ki++){
            var key=String(ks[ki]).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&');
            var rr=new RegExp('["\']?'+key+'["\']?\\s*[:=]\\s*["\']([\\s\\S]{8,4000}?)["\']\\s*(?:[,}])','i');

        }

    }
    function qfApplyDetailHtml301(html){

    /* alpha30.4：总推荐/评分不再只依赖 APP BookDetail。当前起点官网书页本身会服务端输出
       “xxx万字 xxx万总推荐 xxx周推荐”，并可能在 hydration JSON 中携带 BssRecomTotal /
       BssReadTotal / OutCircleIndexInfo.Score。先精确解析当前 result；仍缺字段时只额外请求一次
       https://www.qidian.com/book/{bookId}/。 */
    function qfNormHtml304(html){
        return String(html||'').replace(/&quot;/gi,'"').replace(/&#34;/g,'"').replace(/\\"/g,'"').replace(/\u0022/gi,'"').replace(/\u003c/gi,'<').replace(/\u003e/gi,'>');
    }
    function qfScalar304(html,keys){
        var s=qfNormHtml304(html),ks=keys||[];
        for(var i304=0;i304<ks.length;i304++){
            var key304=String(ks[i304]).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&');
            var rr304=[
                new RegExp('["\']?'+key304+'["\']?\\s*[:=]\\s*["\']?([0-9]+(?:\\.[0-9]+)?(?:万)?)','i'),
                new RegExp('\\\"'+key304+'\\\"\\s*:\s*\\\"?([0-9]+(?:\\.[0-9]+)?(?:万)?)','i')
            ];
            for(var j304=0;j304<rr304.length;j304++){var m304=s.match(rr304[j304]);if(m304&&m304[1])return String(m304[1]);}
        }
        return '';
    }
    function qfScore304(html){
        var s=qfNormHtml304(html),blocks=[/OutCircleIndexInfo["']?\s*:\s*\{([\s\S]{0,2400}?)\}/i,/outCircleIndexInfo["']?\s*:\s*\{([\s\S]{0,2400}?)\}/i];
        for(var i304=0;i304<blocks.length;i304++){
            var b304=s.match(blocks[i304]);if(!b304||!b304[1])continue;
            var mm304=b304[1].match(/["']?(?:Score|score)["']?\s*:\s*["']?([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)/);
            if(mm304&&mm304[1])return String(mm304[1]);
        }
        return qfScalar304(s,['BookScore','bookScore','RatingScore','ratingScore','BookStar','bookStar']);
    }

/* alpha30.5：统计纠错层。
 * ① 收藏/粉丝类字段在当前 APP 响应中的语义并不稳定，已出现 15 / 327 这类明显错误值，详情页暂不展示；
 * ② 总推荐增加起点官方搜索页兜底，并且必须用当前 bookId 锚定结果块，杜绝抓到其他书；
 * ③ 评分只保留 0~10 的真实数值，拿不到就隐藏，不猜测。 */
try{
    function qfCount305(v){
        var s=String(v==null?'':v).replace(/,/g,'').replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,'');
        if(!s)return '';
        var m=s.match(/^([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)(万)?$/);if(!m)return '';
        return m[1]+(m[2]?'万':'');
    }
    function qfAnchorSearch305(html){
        html=String(html||'');if(!html||!bid)return '';
        var marks=['/book/'+String(bid)+'/','/book/'+String(bid)+'"','bookId='+String(bid),'data-bid="'+String(bid)+'"',"data-bid='"+String(bid)+"'"];
        var ix=-1;for(var mi=0;mi<marks.length;mi++){ix=html.indexOf(marks[mi]);if(ix>=0)break;}
        if(ix<0)return '';
        var st=Math.max(0,ix-5000),ed=Math.min(html.length,ix+14000);
        return html.slice(st,ed);
    }
    function qfSearchRecommend305(html){
        var sn=qfAnchorSearch305(html);if(!sn)return '';
        var plain=sn.replace(/<script[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi,' ').replace(/<style[\s\S]*?<\/style>/gi,' ').replace(/<[^>]+>/g,' ').replace(/&nbsp;/gi,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ');

        if(m&&m[1])return String(m[1]).replace(/,/g,'')+(m[2]?'万':'');
        /* 搜索页改版时再尝试当前结果块里的结构化字段。 */
        var ks=['BssRecomTotal','RecommendAll','TotalRecommend','TotalRecommendCount','recommendCount'];
        for(var i=0;i<ks.length;i++){
            var kk=ks[i].replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&');
            var rr=new RegExp('["\\\']?'+kk+'["\\\']?\\s*[:=]\\s*["\\\']?([0-9]+(?:\\.[0-9]+)?)','i');
            var mm=sn.match(rr);if(mm&&mm[1])return mm[1];
        }

    }

try{
    function qfAbsNum306(v){
        var s=String(v==null?'':v).replace(/,/g,'').replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,'');if(!s)return '';
        var m=s.match(/^([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*(w|W|k|K|万|千)?$/);if(!m)return '';
        var n=Number(m[1]);if(!isFinite(n)||n<0)return '';
        var u=m[2]||'';if(u==='w'||u==='W'||u==='万')n*=10000;else if(u==='k'||u==='K'||u==='千')n*=1000;
        return String(Math.round(n));
    }
    function qfPlain306(html){
        return String(html||'').replace(/<script[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi,' ').replace(/<style[\s\S]*?<\/style>/gi,' ').replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi,'\n').replace(/<\/p>/gi,'\n').replace(/<[^>]+>/g,' ').replace(/&nbsp;/gi,' ').replace(/&amp;/gi,'&').replace(/&#39;/gi,"'").replace(/&quot;/gi,'"').replace(/[ \t]+/g,' ').replace(/\n\s+/g,'\n');

    }
